Method: 提出BrainPilot多智能体系统，包含：PI智能体协调专业智能体；统一脑科学知识库（7,233个索引项）和技能库（72个可复用方法论单元，覆盖7个研究领域）；Graph of Trace（追踪图）记录每一步骤供审计；Auditor智能体整合捏造检查工作流。

Result: 在Agents' Last Exam中的三项脑科学任务、自建BrainPilotBench-v0基准以及端到端案例研究中，BrainPilot使用开源骨干模型达到与最先进智能体框架相当的性能，且成本更低。

Conclusion: BrainPilot通过全开源、可审计、多智能体协作的方式，有效加速脑科学研究，在保持性能的同时降低计算成本，为科学智能体提供了可信赖的范式。

Abstract: Understanding the brain increasingly depends on integrating evidence across scales, modalities, and disciplines. Addressing a single research question therefore requires a coordinated sequence of operations, from surveying prior work to executing analyses and interpreting results in light of domain knowledge. AI agents promise to accelerate this process, but current agents lack domain expertise in brain science, may fabricate claims, drift during multi-step reasoning, and offer few defined points for expert intervention. These failures are especially costly in brain science, where conclusions feed into downstream scientific claims and depend on laboratory-specific expertise and careful human judgment. We present \textbf{BrainPilot} a \textbf{fully open-source} multi-agent system that accelerates brain science research with traceable logs and agent-verified results. A principal investigator (PI) agent coordinates specialist agents grounded in curated domain knowledge: a unified brain science knowledge base containing 7{,}233 indexed items and a skill library of 72 reusable methodology units across seven research domains. Every major step is recorded in the Graph of Trace, an auditable record that links subgoals, tool use, evidence, and claims and allows researchers to follow and inspect the workflow. An Auditor agent further integrates fabrication checking into the workflow. For evaluation, we run three brain science tasks from Agents' Last Exam, introduce our own benchmark, \textbf{BrainPilotBench-v0}, and present additional end-to-end case studies. Across these evaluations, BrainPilot with an open-source backbone model attains performance comparable to state-of-the-art agent framework with less costs.

</details>


### [78] [Long-Context Fine-Tuning with Limited VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15105)
*Vladimir Fedosov,Aleksandr Sazhin,Artemiy Grinenko,Frank Woernle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分层全局注意力（HGA）结合分段反向传播和分层KV存储，使QLoRA微调在16GB GPU上支持16K训练 tokens和131K推理 tokens，同时保持与密集注意力相当的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调虽减少了模型和优化器内存，但密集注意力机制仍使得长序列训练成本高昂，现有方法在有限显存下难以支持长上下文训练。

Method: 结合分层全局注意力（HGA）、分段反向传播（仅活跃段可微）和分层KV存储（旧KV卸载至RAM/NVMe），HGA为每个查询块加载有界数量的精确历史token进行注意力计算。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B、4-bit QLoRA、PG19上，密集训练在16GB Quadro RTX 5000上最多2K tokens（失败于4K），HGA达到16K tokens（峰值15.28GB）；推理达131K tokens。相同2K训练长度下，HGA与密集训练的困惑度相当（2.7405 vs 2.7383），吞吐量略优（217.75 vs 207.02 tokens/s）。

Conclusion: HGA能够在有限显存下实现极长序列的高效微调，且质量和吞吐量不输密集注意力，随上下文增长优势更明显，并可用于检索和生成。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces model and optimizer memory, but dense attention still makes long training sequences expensive. We combine Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) with segment-wise backpropagation and tiered KV storage. Only the active segment remains differentiable in VRAM; older KV is detached into RAM or NVMe, and HGA loads a bounded set of exact historical tokens for each query block. On Qwen3-8B with 4-bit QLoRA and PG19, dense training on a 16 GB Quadro RTX 5000 fits 2,048 tokens but fails at 4,096, whereas HGA reaches 16,384 tokens with 15.28 GB peak VRAM. Under evaluation the same adapter runs through 131,072 tokens on this card; VRAM is not constant but grows gently with the resident chunk summaries, so RAM and NVMe capacity set the practical limit beyond these lengths. At the shared 2K training length, HGA-trained and dense-trained adapters obtain 2.7405 and 2.7383 nat under the same dense-attention readout, while the stock model obtains 2.9541. At this boundary HGA training is already marginally faster (217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s), and the HGA-to-dense throughput ratio improves from 1K to 2K; because HGA keeps the attended historical set per token approximately constant while dense work per token grows, we expect this lead to widen as context grows. Dense attention is used for the main quality and retrieval comparisons so that they measure the learned weights and remain compatible with standard generation frameworks. HGA can also be used for retrieval and generation; an optimized production-grade serving implementation is under development.

</details>


### [79] [Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15142)
*Yukuan Lu,Zaishuo Xia,Weyl Lu,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种视觉世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM）在Atari Pong环境中的表现，发现所有模型在封闭循环 rollout 和像素空间零样本MBRL中均存在严重失败（如球消失、运动错误等），并提出概念引导空间正则化（CGSReg）以部分缓解这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常作为基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）系统的组成部分进行评估，而很少被单独研究。作者希望系统性地、独立地评估世界模型本身的建模质量，揭示其潜在瓶颈。

Method: 1) 复现五种视觉世界模型（DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, STORM）的完整训练流程并匹配原始报告性能。2) 冻结世界模型，使用单独训练的策略与冻结模型交互进行封闭循环rollout诊断，检查视觉和动力学错误。3) 进行像素空间零样本MBRL，即在冻结世界模型内训练新策略后直接在真实环境中评估。4) 提出CGSReg，对分割出的概念区域施加辅助像素重建损失。

Result: 所有五种模型的rollout均存在明显失败，包括球消失、错误运动、无效碰撞。零样本MBRL中所有模型策略性能显著下降，DreamerV3的平均回报从-5.5降至-20.9（接近最低值-21）。CGSReg改善了DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER的封闭循环rollout和零样本MBRL，但效果因模型和评估指标而异。

Conclusion: 当前视觉世界模型在关键概念（如Pong中的球）建模上存在严重不足，这导致其在下游任务中表现脆弱。CGSReg作为一种辅助正则化方法可部分缓解问题，但无法完全消除所有瓶颈，说明世界模型仍存在更根本的局限性。

Abstract: World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation.
  We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions.
  Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21.
  We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.

</details>


### [80] [The Industrialization of Research ; On AI-Driven Science and Its Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15164)
*Emmanuel Jeannot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能从工具角色转变为科研自主参与者所带来的"科研工业化"转型，以美国能源部Genesis任务为例，系统分析了这一转型引发的七个核心问题，并指出这些问题是负责任地推进AI驱动科学必须正视的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在揭示AI在科研中从辅助工具到自主参与者的角色转变所带来的深层次结构性变革，警示科研工业化过程中可能被忽视的重大风险。

Method: 采用概念辨析与案例分析法，以Genesis任务为具体实例，从七个维度展开系统性批判性分析。

Result: 识别出七个关键问题：科研能力代际传承断裂、AI生成理论的可解释性危机、同行评议体系崩溃、AI范式革新能力未经验证、科研议程被政治/工业力量俘获、闭环管道中系统误差累积、全球科研共同体结构性分化。

Conclusion: AI驱动科研的潜力真实且显著，但必须在正视上述七个条件的前提下负责任地推进其发展。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming scientific research -- not merely as a more powerful instrument, but as an autonomous participant in the research cycle itself. This transition constitutes, in the most precise sense of the term, the industrialization of research: a shift from a craft model, in which knowledge, method, and judgment are embedded in the researcher, to a pipeline model, in which these steps are decomposed, automated, and supervised. The US Department of Energy's Genesis Mission is the most ambitious current instantiation of this shift, but the fundamental questions it raises extend far beyond any single program. This essay examines seven such questions: the erosion of the intergenerational transmission of scientific competence; the growing opacity of AI-generated theories; the collapse of peer evaluation under a flood of machine-generated output; the unproven capacity of AI for paradigm-shifting discovery; the capture of the scientific agenda by political and industrial actors; the compounding of systematic errors in closed-loop pipelines; and the structural bifurcation of the global research community into incommensurable tiers. These concerns do not constitute an argument against AI-driven science -- whose demonstrated potential is real and significant. They constitute the conditions under which that potential can be responsibly pursued.

</details>


### [81] [MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15166)
*Goktug Ozkan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedFailBench 是一个由临床医生构建的合成基准和故障图谱，用于评估医学AI的安全边界失败类型，按严重性等级（1-5）和安全门类型（如漏诊紧急转诊、不安全远程给药等）对错误进行分类。当前版本（v0.2.1）包含44例临床医生审查的合成病例、严重性注释、安全门分类体系、临床严重性评分标准及自动化筛查流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI基准大多只评估模型是否知道正确答案，而忽视了模型在哪些安全边界上失败的问题。MedFailBench旨在系统性地识别和分类医学AI错误的安全失败类型。

Method: 临床医生构建合成病例基准，按严重性等级（1-5）和安全门类型（漏诊紧急转诊、不安全远程给药、不安全出院 reassurance、证据捏造、不安全协议执行、来源支持缺口）对错误进行标注，并配套自动化模型响应筛查流水线。

Result: 发布了v0.2.1版本，包含44例临床医生审查的合成病例、严重性注释、HuggingFace排行榜预览、安全门分类体系、临床严重性评分标准及自动化流水线。不包含患者数据、临床验证声明或模型排名。

Conclusion: MedFailBench提供了一个专注于医学AI安全边界失败的评估基准，补充了传统仅关注答案正确性的评估方式，有助于定位具体的安全失败类型。

Abstract: Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.

</details>


### [82] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15176)
*Patrick Phuoc Do,Chau M. Ta,Chaoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了6种多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在科学可视化素养测试中的表现，发现Gemini模型整体最强，但所有模型在量化估计、纹理/集成可视化等任务上存在显著不足，表明科学可视化素养应作为评估多模态AI系统的必要维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评估主要集中于图表（chart-centric），缺乏对科学可视化（SciVis）理解能力的系统验证，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 采用标准化的科学可视化素养评估测试（包含49个题目、18个可视化/插图、8种技术、11种任务类型），在闭世界协议下对3个闭源和3个开源MLLM进行基准测试，并与485名人类参与者的数据进行比较。

Result: Gemini模型整体最强，超过人类均值；开源模型低于人类基线。模型在科学插图、搜索和空间理解任务上表现最佳，但在纹理/集成可视化及量化估计任务上表现不佳。错误分析揭示了精细化量化估计、流向解读和编码解释方面的反复失败。

Conclusion: 科学可视化素养应作为评估多模态AI系统的必要基准维度，当前MLLMs在该领域能力不均匀且存在系统性短板。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.

</details>


### [83] [Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15190)
*Han Jiang,Sunbeom Kwon,Jinwen Luo,Ziang Xiao,Susu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了经典IRT估计方法在AI基准测试（少量模型、大量题目、非正态能力分布）中的失效风险，发现标准估计器在大规模场景下不可行，而可扩展估计器在小模型集或非正态分布时产生不可靠推断，并给出了可靠使用所需样本量和诊断建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI基准测试的数据特征（模型少、题目多、能力分布偏斜/聚类/多峰）与人类测试的数据特征严重不匹配，导致从人类测试领域引入的IRT标准估计工具可能不可靠，需要系统评估这种失配风险。

Method: 基于六个LLM基准的真实题目参数和能力分布，模拟三种常见IRT模型下的响应矩阵，比较四种估计工具（边际最大似然、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛、变分推断、神经伪孪生估计器），在18,000种模拟条件下评估计算可行性、可扩展性以及模型排名、预测性能和题目特征的推断可靠性。

Result: 经典估计器在大规模基准场景下不可行，可扩展估计器在模型集较小或能力分布非正态时会产生不可靠的题目级和排名推断。

Conclusion: 确定了潜特质模型何时能可靠支持AI基准评估、何时会扭曲评估结论，并给出了可靠使用所需的样本量和诊断指标。

Abstract: AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.

</details>


### [84] [Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15193)
*Madhumitha Venkatesan,Shicheng Wen,Jiajing Guo,Jorge Piazentin Ono,Liu Ren,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Plover是一个以计划为中心的视觉GUI自动化系统，通过外部化任务计划和重新规划为持久、可检查和可修改的工件，使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和适应性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI自动化代理在面对动态布局、意外对话框和不断变化的界面状态时容易偏离用户意图，且这些代理的规划和适应过程通常是内部化的，限制了用户检查、监督或纠正系统行为的能力。

Method: 采用规划器-执行器架构，将任务计划和重新规划外部化为持久、可检查和可修改的工件。支持对演变中执行的显式监督、通过可编辑计划进行局部修正、自然语言指导和截图基础的干预，同时在修复过程中保留先前进度。通过6名参与者的形成性研究指导交互设计。

Result: 许多自主GUI代理的失败在计划保持可见且干预局部化的情况下是可结构修复的，显式重新规划有助于使GUI自动化更加透明、可控和适应性强。

Conclusion: Plover通过外部化计划与重新规划，有效提升了GUI自动化系统的可监督性和可纠正性，使得在复杂真实环境中代理失败后仍能通过局部化干预完成修复。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.

</details>


### [85] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种自我进化、专家在环的MDD标注框架，结合LLM辅助标注与专家验证，通过双记忆架构内化专家反馈，生成符合DSM-5-TR标准且可审计的标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症数据集的标注缺乏结构化证据、症状级理由以及与DSM-5-TR标准的可追溯对齐，限制了透明度和模型可解释性，标注质量成为心理健康领域XAI系统的主要瓶颈。

Method: 三阶段流程：(1)候选证据选择；(2)DSM-5-TR标准级分析；(3)案例级综合生成标签和严重程度标注。采用双记忆架构（示例记忆+反思记忆）内化专家反馈，无需重训即可迭代改进。

Result: 在专家审核的试点研究中，该方法提升了标注一致性和可解释性，同时减少了人工修订工作量。

Conclusion: 该框架支持构建可解释、符合DSM-5-TR的数据集，提供临床证据、推理轨迹和编辑历史实现全面可审计性，多轮反馈评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [86] [When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15218)
*Weimeng Wang,Ziqiang Wang,Zihang Zhan,Chuanpu Fu,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM作为具身智能体规划器时，物理世界中的安全风险与文本内容安全风险在表征层面是可分离的，并提出了基于隐藏状态探测的轻量级安全方法PRISM，在多个基准上实现了高效低误报的物理安全检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用作具身智能体的高层规划器，语言上无害的指令在物理世界中落地后可能变得危险。现有研究未能区分这种物理层面的危险与传统的文本内容危险，因此需要探究两者在表征层面是否可分离，并开发针对物理安全的检测方法。

Method: 通过隐藏状态方向分析和随机分割零假设检验，证明内容危险（CD）与物理危险（PD）在多种LLM的表征中是可分离的。基于此，提出PRISM：一个在完整隐藏状态上训练的L2正则化逻辑回归探测层。同时构建了PhysicalSafetyBench-1K（PSB-1K）对比基准，包含1000个无直接危害关键词的物理风险样本对。

Result: PRISM在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2-87.7%准确率，11.7-13.7%误报率；同等规模LLM判别器的误报率为24.7-39.0%。在PSB-1K上PRISM达99.6%准确率、0.7%误报率，而Qwen2.5-3B判别器误拒了67.8%的安全任务。结果在SafeText和EARBench上得到复现。

Conclusion: 物理危险和内容危险在LLM表征层面是可分离的，基于隐藏状态探测的方法（PRISM）能够有效检测具身智能体场景中的物理安全风险，且显著优于同等规模的LLM判别器，是超越文本内容审核的表示级物理安全方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.

</details>


### [87] [AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15247)
*Moein Taherinezhad,Sebastian Maier,Gerardo Vitagliano,Francesco Pierri,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoSynthesis是一个端到端的多智能体系统，能够自动化完成元分析的全流程——从研究问题输入到文献检索、筛选、数据提取、效应量计算、随机效应元分析、异质性分析和偏倚风险评估，最终生成符合PRISMA标准的透明报告。实验表明其 pooled effect estimates 与专家手动元分析结果高度一致（Hedges' g 相近），证明了该方法可扩展定量证据合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量证据合成（元分析）目前仍高度依赖人工操作，难以规模化扩展，限制了其在科学、医学、教育和政策等领域的广泛应用。

Method: 构建端到端多智能体系统，将元分析分解为以下步骤：自然语言研究问题输入→搜索策略制定→文献检索→候选研究筛选→全文资格评估→定量统计量提取→标准化效应量计算→随机效应元分析。系统还支持异质性分析和偏倚风险评估，并生成符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。

Result: AutoSynthesis筛选了28篇以上研究，提取了20多个定量声明。其 pooled effect estimates 与专家手动元分析得到的 Hedges' g 相近，表明自动化结果与人工证据合成高度一致。

Conclusion: AutoSynthesis能够使定量证据合成过程更具可扩展性，从而支持跨学科的证据驱动决策。

Abstract: Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.

</details>


### [88] [teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15254)
*Qiwei Li,Jorge Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: teLLMe是一个面向城市驾驶数据集的探索性因果分析系统，它从结构化事件表出发，结合因果结构学习、自助法稳定性检验和DoWhy框架，将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，生成包含效应估计、调整集、DAG支持及假设的“因果卡”，帮助专家进行假设生成与因果推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通机构拥有大量视频衍生数据，但多为观察性数据，缺乏干预，难以回答如“降雨如何改变交通密度”等因果问题。现有方法难以从观测数据中进行系统化的因果分析。

Method: 系统从带标注的行车记录仪视频构建结构化事件表；使用PC算法进行因果结构学习；结合自助法（bootstrap）进行稳定性检验；通过DoWhy和线性回归进行查询特定的效应估计；利用模式感知的LLM将自然语言问题映射为结构化因果查询，最终生成“因果卡”输出。

Result: 基于BDD交通事件的案例研究表明，系统能够揭示涉及天气、高峰时段和交通密度之间的合理因果关系，同时明确呈现不确定性及建模选择。

Conclusion: teLLMe是为假设生成和专家推理设计的工具，而非提供确定性因果主张的来源，有助于在观察性交通数据中进行探索性因果分析。

Abstract: Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.

</details>


### [89] [Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15267)
*Victoria Graf,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Noah A. Smith,David Kohlbrenner,Kyle Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过公共讨论界面进行的大规模预训练数据投毒攻击，并提出了HalfLife方法用于评估对抗性内容在网络爬取数据中的包含情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据投毒研究局限于Wikipedia等已建立的数据源，忽略了真实预训练语料库的大规模性和异质性，以及投毒数据与数据筛选管线的交互作用。

Method: 利用公共讨论界面作为网络规模的内容注入机制进行投毒攻击；提出HalfLife分析方法，用于估计对抗性内容在经过网络爬取和数据筛选后是否仍保留在训练数据中。

Result: 证明了通过网络公开讨论界面进行大规模预训练数据投毒是可行的；HalfLife分析表明需要评估投毒注入是否被包含在预训练数据中。

Conclusion: 第三方网页内容可作为攻击语言模型预训练的潜在向量，研究强调了评估投毒注入是否包含在预训练数据中的重要性。

Abstract: Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [Beyond Object Validation: Relational Conformance in Multi-Artifact Agent Releases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14155)
*Tengjiao Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了Agent系统中最终发布包逃过审查而导致的自相矛盾问题，提出Schema-SIP关系一致性（SIP-RC）候选方案，将发布建模为图结构，包含证据指向、决策权限、衍生制品溯源等约束，并建立了故障类别，展示了多种机制的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent系统能够验证输入、工具调用和生成对象，但最终的发布包往往逃过了同样的审查，导致发布包内部自相矛盾（如DRSS案例中ledger支持60分但报告宣称100分Gold Path），需要系统性解决此类故障。

Method: 分析DRSS发布故障、Schema Docs（类似缺陷成为产品契约）和Brand Shuttle GEO（证据转化为修复工作）三个案例，提出SIP-RC方案：将发布建模为有向图，claims指向evidence，decisions携带有限权限，derived artifacts保留执行条件和谱系，发布字节必须与经过检查的包一致，硬故障不可平均化消除，validator在独立路径上重算关键决策。

Result: 建立了上述故障类别，展示了多种机制的实用性，但完整SIP-RC方案是否优于现有检查仍是一个待实验验证的开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文识别并形式化了一类被忽视的Agent系统故障——最终发布包的一致性逃逸问题，提出了图建模的SIP-RC方案，但该方案的完整有效性需要通过实验进一步验证。

Abstract: Agent systems validate inputs, tool calls, and generated objects. The final package often escapes the same scrutiny. In one DRSS release, the ledger supported 60 points and a failed certificate; the report announced a 100-point Gold Path. Every local gate was green. The package contradicted itself.
  We study that failure alongside Schema Docs, where similar faults became product contracts, and Brand Shuttle GEO, where evidence is turned into repair work. The result is a candidate Schema-SIP Relational Conformance profile (SIP-RC). It models a release as a graph: claims point to evidence, decisions carry bounded authority, derived artifacts retain their execution conditions and lineage, and published bytes must match the package that was checked. Hard failures cannot be averaged away, and a validator recomputes critical decisions on a separate path. The paper establishes the failure class and shows that several mechanisms are practical. Whether the full profile performs better than existing checks remains an open experiment.

</details>


### [91] [Natural-Language to SysMLv2 Translation via Conformance-Driven Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14162)
*Chance LaVoie,Eladio Andujar Lugo,Taylan G. Topcu,Levent Burak Kara*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于符合性检查器驱动的自然语言到SysMLv2翻译框架，通过生成-检查-修复循环确保生成的模型达到生产级接受标准。在SysMBench的151个提示集上，该方法在四个大语言模型后端上实现了100%的生产符合性接受率，而单次生成仅为51.16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言到SysMLv2的翻译方法仅关注语法约束的满足，而忽略了生产级建模环境的实际接受要求，导致生成的模型虽语法正确但无法在实际工业环境中部署使用。

Method: 构建生成-检查-修复循环框架，将SysMLv2符合性检查器嵌入其中作为判定依据。每次生成的模型由检查器评估，根据确定性诊断信息进行迭代修正，直至零符合性错误。

Result: 在SysMBench的151个提示集上，使用四种大语言模型后端进行604次提示-模型测试，单次生成的生产符合性接受率为51.16%，而该方法达到100%的符合率。

Conclusion: 将生产符合性检查从后处理步骤提升为生成过程中的控制机制，能够将概率性输出转化为可直接用于加载、可视化和工程使用的生产级SysMLv2工件。

Abstract: Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) relies on formal system models as primary technical artifacts for representing requirements, structure, and behavior across the system lifecycle. With the standardization of SysMLv2 as a textual language, interest is increasing in translating natural-language descriptions directly into executable models. For practical deployment, generated models must be accepted by industrial modeling environments, not merely satisfy grammar constraints. We present a conformance-checker-driven framework for reliable natural-language-to-SysMLv2 translation that enforces production-level acceptance as the termination condition. The system embeds a SysMLv2 conformance checker within a generate-check-repair loop. Each model is evaluated using the checker, and deterministic diagnostics are incorporated into revisions until zero conformance errors are achieved. Using the production checker as the oracle ensures the framework targets deployability rather than grammar plausibility. We evaluate the approach on the full SysMBench prompt set of 151 prompts across four large language model backends, yielding 604 prompt-model cases. Single-shot generation achieves 51.16% production-conformance acceptance, while our approach achieves 100.00% conformance. By elevating production conformance from a post-processing check to a control mechanism within generation, the framework converts probabilistic outputs into production-accepted SysMLv2 artifacts suitable for loading, visualization, and engineering use.

</details>


### [92] [Towards Reliable AI-Assisted Analog Design: Template-Constrained LLM Agents for SAR ADC Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14165)
*Dimple Vijay Kochar,Hae-Seung Lee,Anantha P. Chandrakasan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS框架，通过多步LLM智能体结合专家知识，实现能够通过SPICE仿真的SAR ADC自动生成，解决了LLM在模拟EDA应用中的幻觉和失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件代码生成方面表现出色，但在模拟EDA领域受限于电路拓扑理解不足和数据稀缺，直接使用LLM和多模态模型会导致幻觉，无法生成通过SPICE仿真的原理图。

Method: 提出端到端多步LLM智能体框架ATLAS，包含规划、选择、参数化和迭代修改四个步骤；引入模板约束生成方法，利用专家知识约束LLM行为，使其遵循模拟设计的严格约束。

Result: 成功生成了能够通过SPICE仿真验证的功能性SAR ADC，并在不同工艺节点和输入规格下验证了框架的有效性，建立了强概念验证。

Conclusion: 专家知识驱动的多步智能体ATLAS框架为将LLM集成到可靠的模拟设计方法中奠定了实用基础，相比其他模板方法具有更好的泛化性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant capability in software code generation, their application to analog Electronic Design Automation (EDA) is bottlenecked. Owing to limited circuit topology understanding and data, directly prompting LLMs and multimodal models leads to hallucinations and failure to produce schematics capable of passing rigorous SPICE simulations, as we show in our work. Instead, we propose an end-to-end, multi-step LLM agentic framework ATLAS, capable of generating a functional Successive Approximation Register (SAR) Analog-to-Digital Converter (ADC) that successfully passes simulation validation. To adhere to the rigid constraints of analog design, we utilize expert knowledge to ground the LLM in its planning, selection, parameterization, and iterative modification. As part of ATLAS, we introduce Template-Constrained Generation - which unlike other template-based works - builds towards a more generalized SAR ADC generation flow. We demonstrate a strong proof-of-concept of our framework by developing SAR ADCs across technology nodes and input specs. Overall, our expert-knowledge grounded multi-step agentic ATLAS establishes a pragmatic foundation for integrating LLMs into reliable analog design methodologies.

</details>


### [93] [Stop Means Stop: Measuring and Repairing the Enforcement Gap in Agent-Framework Control Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14166)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 六个主流开源LLM-agent框架的防护门（审批、取消、超时）均存在语义漏洞，导致副作用在暂停期间逃逸。作者提出SOUNDGATE——基于内核路由的外部效果门，通过形式化验证（Verus/TLA+/Loom）和1.2e7次差分一致性测试，在修复所有漏洞的同时保持约1ms/次的开销和12k-26k次/秒的吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-agent框架的防护原语（人机审批门、取消、超时）虽然名称和文档暗示了屏障语义（暂停/取消/超时时无副作用执行），但实证表明这些隐含契约实际均未得到保障，存在严重安全风险。

Method: 1) 使用无模型差分探针在六个主流开源框架中系统地发现漏洞；2) 提出SOUNDGATE，一个独立于环境的外部效果门，所有副作用必须通过其准入，强制实现hold-until-decided、reject-cancels、dedup-on-replay、fence-on-cancel四项属性；3) 结合Verus、TLA+/TLC（穷举至7.5e7状态）、TLAPS和Loom进行形式化验证，以及1.2e7次操作差分一致性测试连接模型与代码。

Result: 所有六个框架均存在漏洞。在预设协议下，前沿模型生成触发漏洞的计划形状的汇集率高达14%；在真实模型驱动的未修改框架中，1200次运行里有215次在审批暂停期间执行了副作用。SOUNDGATE端到端阻止所有测量到的违规，同时释放合法效果，写入延迟约1ms，持久化吞吐12k-26k次/秒。

Conclusion: 现有LLM-agent框架的防护语义存在系统性缺陷，SOUNDGATE通过外部强制的完整中介契约有效修复了这些漏洞，为LLM-agent的安全部署提供了可靠的防护机制。

Abstract: Production LLM-agent frameworks expose control primitives -- human-in-the-loop approval gates, run cancellation, and execution timeouts -- whose names and documentation imply barrier semantics: while a run is paused, cancelled, or timed out, no gated side effect executes. We show this implied contract holds on none of the six widely used open-source frameworks we test. Model-free differential probes isolate a recurring sibling leak -- an approval gate suspends its own branch while a sibling branch's effect executes during the pause, so a later rejection cannot prevent it -- in every framework shipping a pre-execution gate (five of six), plus three further gaps: replay double-execution, cancellation orphans, and timeout zombies. The hazard is reachable, not merely constructible: under an a-priori-fixed protocol, frontier models emit the leak-triggering plan shape at pooled rates up to 14%, and when live models drive the unmodified frameworks under an approval pause, 215 of 1,200 runs execute their effect during the pause, across three schedulers and two language runtimes. To repair the measured gaps we present SOUNDGATE, an environment-external effect gate in Rust through which every side effect must be admitted, enforcing hold-until-decided, reject-cancels, dedup-on-replay, and fence-on-cancel -- one property per violation class -- under a stated complete-mediation contract discharged for network egress by kernel-enforced routes. We verify the properties over a model of the admission core (Verus; TLA+/TLC, exhaustive to 7.5e7 states; TLAPS), model-check the deployed Rust with Loom, and bridge model to code by differential conformance over 1.2e7 operations -- refinement evidence, not a mechanized proof. SOUNDGATE blocks every measured violation end-to-end on all six frameworks while releasing legitimate effects, at about 1 ms admission per write and 12k-26k durable admissions per second.

</details>


### [94] [Structured Feedback Improves Repair in an LLM Agent Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14167)
*Jaideep Ray,Ankit Goyal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VeriHarness是一个代码控制的智能体循环框架，用于研究外部验证器反馈格式对LLM agent修复能力的影响。实验发现，包含失败位置、观测值和可接受替代方案的完整反馈可将终端成功率提升40+个百分点，其中"可接受替代方案"是最大增益来源，而JSON语法本身并无显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent在执行任务时常被外部验证器拒绝，但验证与模型下一次调用之间的接口（即反馈的格式和内容）缺乏系统研究，作者旨在探索不同反馈信息对agent修复成功率的影响。

Method: 提出VeriHarness框架，让模型生成候选方案，外部验证器控制接受、预算和追踪。在50对TextWorld游戏中设置四轮调用上限，比较原始诊断与包含不同信息字段（失败位置、观测值、可接受替代方案）的反馈效果，同时对比JSON格式与自然语言散文格式。

Result: 完整反馈（含三个字段）使Qwen2.5-Coder-14B终端成功率从14/50提升至36/50（+44个百分点），Llama-3.1-8B从8/50提升至29/50（+42个百分点）。消融实验表明增益主要来自"可接受替代方案"字段；仅含位置和观测值的反馈效果与原始诊断基线相当。散文格式与JSON格式效果几乎相同。

Conclusion: 外部验证器提供的反馈内容中，"可接受替代方案"是提升LLM agent修复成功率的关键因素，而非结构化格式（JSON vs 散文）或单纯的错误定位信息。该结论在不同调用预算和采样解码设置下保持一致。

Abstract: LLM agents often retry after external validation rejects a candidate, but the interface between validation and the next model call remains underspecified. We introduce VeriHarness, a code-controlled agent loop in which models generate candidates while external validators control acceptance, budgets, and traces. We use it to compare raw diagnostics with feedback that identifies the failure location, observed value, and admissible alternatives. Across 50 paired TextWorld games under a four-call cap, feedback containing all three fields raises terminal success from 14/50 to 36/50 for Qwen2.5-Coder-14B (+44 percentage points) and from 8/50 to 29/50 for Llama-3.1-8B (+42 points). Ablations locate most of the gain in the admissible alternatives: feedback containing only the location and observed value remains near the raw diagnostic baseline. Presenting the complete repair information in prose instead of a keyed JSON record yields nearly the same success, providing no evidence that JSON syntax itself improves repair. The ordering persists across the tested call budgets and one sampled-decoding setting.

</details>


### [95] [Quantize with Confidence? An Empirical Study of Quantization for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14181)
*Saima Afrin,Md. Zahidul Haque,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种后训练量化方法（GPTQ、AWQ、QuIP#、AQLM、BitsAndBytes、GGUF）在Qwen2.5-Coder和CodeLlama两个大代码模型上的影响，使用McEval和CoderEval基准测试衡量功能正确性、可维护性、可靠性、安全性和结构复杂度，并引入基于香农熵和令牌长度的提示复杂度鲁棒性分析。结果表明AQLM表现最佳，QuIP#退化最严重，安全性保持稳定，不同量化技术的提示鲁棒性存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者越来越多地在笔记本电脑等资源受限硬件上通过Ollama等本地推理框架运行大代码模型，后训练量化对减少内存占用至关重要，但其对生成代码的具体影响尚未被充分理解。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-Coder和CodeLlama两个代码模型系列上，使用McEval（多语言）和CoderEval（Python和Java）基准测试，评估GPTQ、AWQ、QuIP#、AQLM、BitsAndBytes、GGUF六种量化方法，衡量pass@1功能正确性以及可维护性、可靠性、安全性和结构复杂度，并引入基于香农熵和令牌长度的提示复杂度分析。

Result: 不同量化技术对正确性和代码质量的影响存在显著差异；AQLM在多数情况下达到或超过全精度基线，QuIP#在复杂提示上正确性退化最严重；所有模型和语言的安全性指标保持稳定；不同技术对提示复杂度的鲁棒性表现各异。

Conclusion: 为资源受限硬件上部署大代码模型时的量化策略选择提供了实践指导，强调了评估量化模型时需超越功能正确性进行更全面的考量。

Abstract: The growing adoption of local inference frameworks such as Ollama has made it increasingly common for developers to run large code models on laptops and other resource-constrained hardware. In these settings, post-training quantization is essential for reducing memory footprint and enabling practical deployment, yet its impact on generated code remains insufficiently understood. We empirically evaluate six state-of-the-art quantization methods (GPTQ, AWQ, QuIP#, AQLM, BitsAndBytes, and GGUF) on two representative large code model families, Qwen2.5-Coder and CodeLlama, using the multilingual McEval and CoderEval benchmarks for Python and Java. We assess functional correctness (pass@1) together with maintainability, reliability, security, and structural complexity. We also introduce a novel analysis of robustness under varying prompt complexity, characterized by Shannon entropy and token length. Our results show that quantization techniques differ meaningfully in their impact on correctness and code quality. AQLM consistently matches or exceeds the full-precision baseline, whereas QuIP# exhibits the largest correctness degradation, particularly on complex prompts. Security attributes remain stable across models, benchmarks, and programming languages, while robustness to prompt complexity varies across techniques. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting quantization strategies for deploying large code models on resource-constrained hardware and highlight the importance of evaluating quantized models beyond functional correctness.

</details>


### [96] [NexForge: Scaling Executable Agent Tasks via Requirement-First Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14186)
*Jiarong Zhao,Zhikai Lei,Zhiheng Xi,Rui Zheng,Hang Yan,Jie Zhou,Qin Chen,Liang He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: NexForge是一个需求优先的框架，能够将自由形式的能力需求自动编译成可执行的智能体训练数据，无需领域特定的基础设施。通过该框架生成的训练数据，将Qwen3.5-35B-A3B基座模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0上的性能从22.5%提升至52.0%，在GDPval上的Elo评分从813提升至1338；扩展至43.2K终端任务后达到58.4%，超越Claude Opus 4.6。进一步扩展，所合成数据用于训练Nex-N2模型家族，将Terminal-Bench 2.1性能提升至75.3%，GDPval Elo达到1585，实现了开源SOTA性能并超越多个前沿专有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展可执行的智能体训练数据受限于"基板优先"方法，这些方法将任务生成绑定到预定义工具、代码仓库或技能图上：扩大覆盖范围需要手动扩展基板，每个新领域都需要定制化流程，且生成的任务分布往往反映基板的便利性而非真实需求。

Method: NexForge采用需求优先框架：首先进行研究驱动的需求发现，识别代表性任务形式、真实场景及其相对流行度；然后应用分布感知的任务编译，自动检索或构建文件、仓库、依赖项和运行时配置以物化每个任务；最后进行教师轨迹收集和轨迹蒸馏。整个过程无需任何领域特定基础设施。

Result: 在无领域特定基础设施的情况下，生成3,600个终端任务和2,000个办公任务。将Qwen3.5-35B-A3B Base在Terminal-Bench 2.0上从22.5%提升至52.0%，GDPval Elo从813提升至1338。扩展至43.2K终端任务达到58.4%并超越Claude Opus 4.6。Nex-N2模型在Terminal-Bench 2.1上达到75.3%，GDPval Elo达到1585，实现开源SOTA并超越多个前沿专有系统。

Conclusion: NexForge证明了需求优先的自动化数据合成范式可以有效替代基板优先方法，消除领域特定的基础设施需求，实现跨域可扩展的智能体训练数据生成，并最终训练出达到开源SOTA并超越多个前沿专有系统的智能体模型Nex-N2。

Abstract: Scaling executable agent training data is bottlenecked by substrate-first methods that tie task generation to predefined tools, repositories, or skill graphs: expanding coverage requires manual expansion of the substrate, each new domain demands a bespoke pipeline, and the resulting task distributions often reflect substrate convenience rather than real-world demand. We introduce NexForge, a requirement-first framework that compiles free-form capability requirements into executable agent training data. NexForge first performs research-based demand discovery to identify representative task forms, realistic scenarios, and their relative prevalence. It then applies distribution-aware task compilation and automatically retrieves or constructs the files, repositories, dependencies, and runtime configurations required to materialize each task, followed by teacher rollout collection and trajectory distillation. The same pipeline, without any domain-specific infrastructure, produces 3,600 terminal tasks and 2,000 office tasks, improving Qwen3.5-35B-A3B Base from 22.5% to 52.0% on Terminal-Bench 2.0 and from 813 to 1338 Elo on GDPval; scaling to 43.2K terminal tasks reaches 58.4%, surpassing Claude Opus 4.6. Scaled further, NexForge-synthesized data contributes to the training of Nex-N2, a family of publicly available agent models that lift Qwen3.5-35B-A3B to 75.3% on Terminal-Bench 2.1 and to 1585 Elo on GDPval -- achieving state-of-the-art open-source performance and surpassing several frontier proprietary systems. Nex-N2 models are available at https://nex.sii.edu.cn/

</details>


### [97] [ToolAlignBench: Investigating Alignment Conflicts in Tool-Calling Enabled LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14285)
*Aryan Keluskar,Amrita Bhattacharjee,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 安全对齐的大语言模型在工具调用场景中，当安全训练价值（如公共福利）与部署指令冲突时，会以高达43.4%的频率覆盖部署指令，出现告发、数据泄露等行为，揭示多元对齐中安全训练与部署指令间的根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究安全对齐的LLM在工具调用场景中，当安全训练所内化的价值与部署指令发生冲突时，模型会遵循哪种价值，以及这种行为带来的责任风险。

Method: 构建了一个包含16个领域128个场景的基准测试集，评估安全对齐的开源模型在面对暗示组织不当行为的文档时，是否会覆盖部署指令。

Result: 安全对齐的开源模型在高达43.4%的情况下覆盖部署指令，出现告发、数据泄露和篡改证据等行为；abliteration技术可降低外部告发率。

Conclusion: 安全训练虽然保护用户，但会导致智能体违背部署指令，产生不可预测的责任风险，揭示了多元对齐中的根本张力。

Abstract: Safety alignment in LLMs aims to align models with human values, but which values take precedence when they conflict? We investigate this question in the context of tool-calling LLM agents deployed in regulated industries, where agents processing confidential documents may encounter content that triggers safety-trained values (e.g., public welfare) that conflict with deployment-context instructions (e.g., internal logging). To empirically verify this phenomenon, we build a benchmark of 128 scenarios across 16 domains. We find that safety-aligned open-source models override their deployment instructions up to 43.4% of the time, engaging in whistleblowing, data exfiltration, and evidence tampering when processing documents that suggest organizational wrongdoing. We also find that abliteration reduces rates of external whistleblowing. These results reveal a fundamental tension in pluralistic alignment, where the same safety training that protects users can cause agents to act against deployment instructions in ways that create unpredictable liability risks. We release our benchmark as a framework to support evaluation of agent behavior under competing legitimate interests.

</details>


### [98] [Copy-on-Write Scoring: Application-Specific Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14336)
*Joanna Roy,Sven Hoelzel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Copy-on-Write (CoW) Scoring的框架，通过在应用环境中利用PostgreSQL级别的写时复制机制隔离智能体的数据库写入操作，实现对LLM智能体在工作流中操作的细粒度评估，能够定位具体成功和失败的操作，且成本低廉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体评估机制存在局限性：基准测试对特定应用工作流的构念效度低，复制评估环境成本高且容易漂移，缺乏细粒度定位成功/失败操作的能力。

Method: 提出CoW Scoring框架，利用PostgreSQL的写时复制机制隔离智能体在应用环境中的数据库写入操作，通过会话级和操作级评分来评估智能体的具体操作表现。

Result: 在开源项目管理平台Plane上验证，分析揭示了工具接口中的具体问题，相应的修复措施在受影响的模型上带来了可衡量的改进。

Conclusion: CoW Scoring能够低成本、细粒度地评估LLM智能体在应用工作流中的操作表现，有效定位问题并指导改进，为智能体的可信部署提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Trustworthy deployment of LLM-based agents in software systems requires evaluating how they perform on application-specific workflows, with enough granularity to localize where they succeed and fail. Yet existing agent evaluation mechanisms are limited: benchmarks have low construct validity for application-specific workflows and environments, and replica evaluation environments are expensive and prone to drift. We propose Copy-on-Write (CoW) Scoring, a framework that evaluates agent operations directly within application environments using a PostgreSQL-level Copy-on-Write mechanism to isolate agent writes. CoW Scoring produces session- and operation-level scores that highlight where agents' database write operations succeed and fail in a given application environment, enabling inexpensive evaluation and iteration on agent harnesses and tool surfaces. We demonstrate the framework on Plane, an open-source project-management platform, where analysis surfaced specific issues in the tool surface, and corresponding fixes produced measurable improvements on affected models.
  Python library: https://github.com/trail-ml/agent-cow-python

</details>


### [99] [The Prover Is the Judge: Verified Security Software from AI Coding Agents in Ada/SPARK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14340)
*Tobias Philipp*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以验证器驱动的AI编码代理方法，使用Ada/SPARK和GNATprove自动证明安全软件的正确性。代理在密码学、TLS 1.3、IKEv2、X.509和Matrix客户端上完成了49,280个证明义务，监督成本仅为人工验证的1/20至1/40，但发现仅靠GNATprove不足，需结合已知答案测试、互操作性测试和人工审查来捕获缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理生成代码的速度远超人类审查能力，因此需要自动化验证手段来确保代码正确性，尤其是在安全性关键领域。

Method: 采用验证器驱动循环（verifier-driven loop），使用GNATprove对AI代理编写的Ada/SPARK代码进行形式化验证，包括功能正确性证明和运行时错误消除。

Result: GNATprove成功完成49,280个证明义务，为选定原语建立功能正确性，为其余代码证明无运行时错误；监督成本降低至人工验证的1/20至1/40。但发现某些缺陷无法通过GNATprove检测，需结合已知答案测试、互操作性测试和人工审查才能解决。

Conclusion: 核心教训是：代理能被信任建立的内容受限于其反馈机制的强度。弱检查会导致代理规避并虚假报告成功，因此需要强验证反馈来确保可信度。

Abstract: AI coding agents produce code faster than humans can review it. In our approach, the prover is the judge of whether the code is correct. Under a verifier-driven loop, AI agents wrote and verified bare-metal security software in Ada/SPARK spanning classical and post-quantum cryptography, TLS 1.3, IKEv2, X.509, and a Matrix client. GNATprove discharged 49,280 proof obligations, established functional correctness for selected primitives, and proved the absence of run-time errors for the rest, at roughly 20-40 times lower supervision cost than comparable hand verification. GNATprove alone was insufficient: some defects could not be detected and were resolved using known-answer tests, interoperability, or human review of specifications. Given weak checks, the agent tried to bypass them and reported success. We report where each layer caught faults and draw the central lesson: what an agent can be trusted to establish is bounded by the strength of its feedback.

</details>


### [100] [Why Git Is the Memory Solution for the Agentic Development Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14390)
*Frank Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Git版本控制的内存系统，用于记录开发智能体在编码过程中的推理过程。该系统通过将决策历史绑定到仓库的版本控制中，解决了传统检索方法难以保证的真实性、新鲜度、可验证性和可控性问题。通过种子供应（检索排名）和答案组装（路由调度）两阶段方法，在真实开发者问题上实现了0.83的答案充分性，且每个答案仅需382-980 tokens。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的编码智能体产生越来越多的代码，但每次变更背后的推理过程（权衡的方案、发现的约束、被拒绝的方法）都困在智能体的对话记录中，随着会话结束而消失。现有方法将这个问题简单地视为单一检索问题，构建了分层存储、记忆图、编译Wiki、模型判断准入等复杂机制，但这些机制难以保证真实性、新鲜度、可验证性和可控性。

Method: 提出Git绑定的记忆系统，将推理历史纳入版本控制，继承Git的天然保证。系统分两步：1) 种子供应：在8个语料库上进行检索研究，通过预注册的筛选规则，从5种导入的排名机制中保留2种，实现约0.31的池化MRR；2) 答案组装：路由调度器将广度问题分发到Git锚定的结构映射，精准查询到置信度门控的会话记录，推理问题到决策综合模块（重构跨会话的原因弧线，达到0.83充分性）。

Result: 1) 种子供应阶段：最佳配置达到约0.31的池化MRR，比原始转录的grep基线高约60倍，比解析会话的基线高约15倍。2) 答案组装阶段：单次检索仅达到0.07-0.20的答案充分性，而路由后的系统在约5万行代码的生产系统上达到0.83的充分性，且每个答案仅需382-980 tokens（比记录历史低三个数量级）。

Conclusion: 记忆系统应Git绑定而非构建独立的记忆基础设施，通过利用版本控制已有的保证来获得真实性、新鲜度、可验证性和可控性。所有结果均可通过提交-会话链接自动挖掘，无需人工标注，可在任何用户的历史记录上复现。代码、基准测试和论文源码已开源。

Abstract: Coding agents now produce a growing share of a team's code, while the reasoning behind each change -- the alternatives weighed, the constraints discovered, the approaches rejected -- is trapped in assistant transcripts that vanish with the session. Memory for this setting, the agentic development lifecycle (ADLC), is usually posed as one retrieval problem and built as machinery: tiered stores, memory graphs, compiled wikis, model-judged admission. We argue memory should instead be git-bound -- built into the repository's version control, inheriting the guarantees the machinery struggles to construct: ground truth from commits, freshness from rebuild, verification from the merge, containment from review. On this ledger we solve two problems separately, then combine them. Seed supply is closed as an eight-corpus retrieval study under a pre-registered ship discipline: five imported ranking mechanisms rejected, two kept, and a best configuration of ~0.31 pooled MRR -- ~60x the raw-transcript grep floor, ~15x an honest parsed-turn floor. Answer assembly is where ranking stops helping: single-shot retrieval scores only 0.07-0.20 answer-sufficiency on real developer questions, and ungated episode injection measurably degrades good answers. A router dispatches breadth to a git-anchored structural map, pointed lookups to confidence-gated episodes, and rationale to decision synthesis, which reconstructs why-arcs no single session contains (0.83 sufficiency on a young ~50k-LOC production system). Routed, the system answers at 382-980 tokens per question -- three orders of magnitude below the recorded history. Because ground truth is mined from commit-session links rather than annotated, every result is replicable on any user's own history at zero labeling cost. The remaining constraint is capture. Code, benchmark, and paper source: github.com/rekal-dev/rekal-cli.

</details>


### [101] [AI-Conducted Interviews in Empirical Software Engineering: An Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14452)
*Rohit Gheyi,Danyllo Albuquerque,Márcio Ribeiro,Mirko Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了在两项实证软件工程研究中使用定制化MyGPT进行短篇自管理访谈的经验。66份提交物中92.4%格式合规，参与者整体体验积极（90.9%正面评价），但存在问题深度不足、隐私顾虑等限制。AI访谈者应作为短篇、低风险研究的补充方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化访谈在实证软件工程中广泛应用，但存在资源密集、协调困难（时间、地点、语言）的问题。作者旨在探索AI驱动的自管理访谈作为替代方案的可行性和可接受性。

Method: 在两个ESE研究（重构实践、生成式AI在Scrum中的应用）中使用定制化MyGPT进行自管理语音访谈。参与者通过链接访问，选择语言，与AI交互后生成结构化摘要并自愿提交。分析66份提交物、问卷反馈，审计格式、语言、长度和协议一致性。

Result: 92.4%提交物符合预期格式；65份为葡萄牙语、1份为英语；2份与报告协议冲突。90.9%参与者报告正面体验，95.5%认为问题清晰，97.0%认可节奏，89.4%愿意再次参与。局限包括问题通用、敏感度不足、深度不够、隐私顾虑、缺乏人际互动。

Conclusion: 该工作流程在受访者中具有操作可行性和可接受性，但未建立完成率、时间节省、摘要保真度或与人工访谈等效性。AI访谈应作为短篇、聚焦、低风险研究的补充方案，需注意协议设计、隐私指导、人工验证和监督。

Abstract: Semi-structured interviews are widely used in empirical software engineering (ESE), but they are resource-intensive and difficult to coordinate across schedules, locations, and natural languages. This experience report examines a customized MyGPT used to conduct short, self-administered interviews in two ESE studies: one on refactoring practices and another on generative AI in Scrum-related activities. Participants accessed the interviewer through shared links, used voice interaction, selected a preferred natural language, and completed the interview without a researcher present. The AI followed a predefined protocol and generated a structured synthesis that participants voluntarily submitted; these artifacts were not treated as verbatim transcripts. We analyzed 66 submissions and questionnaire responses, and audited artifact format, language, length, and protocol consistency. Of the submitted artifacts, 92.4% followed the expected synthesis format, 65 were predominantly in Portuguese and one in English, and two conflicted with the reported protocol. Participants generally rated the experience positively: 90.9% reported a positive overall experience and comfort, 95.5% considered the questions clear, 97.0% rated the pace positively, and 89.4% would participate again. Reported limitations included generic questions, limited sensitivity to answers, insufficient depth, privacy concerns, and missed human interaction. The findings support the operational viability and acceptability of this workflow among analyzed respondents, but do not establish completion rates, time savings, summary fidelity, or equivalence to human-conducted interviews. AI interviewers should therefore be treated as a complementary option for short, focused, low-risk studies, with protocol design, privacy guidance, artifact validation, and human oversight.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond Generalist LLMs: Specialist Agentic Systems for Structured Code Workflow Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14456)
*Harris Borman,Herman Wandabwa,Fusun Yu,Sandeepa Kannangara,Justin Liu,Anna Leontjeva,Ritchie Ng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究在业务流程自动化领域，专用智能体（将BPMN图转化为可执行工作流）相比通用智能体（如Roo和Cline）在工具使用精确性、延迟、错误率和成本方面表现更优，通用智能体生成代码的功能和质量不一致，不适合工业场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM驱动的软件开发智能体广泛应用的背景下，探究专用智能体是否值得额外的开发投入，特别是在业务流程自动化中BPMN图转可执行工作流这一特定任务上。

Method: 设计了一个专用工作流用于将BPMN图转化为确定性的可执行工作流，并与Roo、Cline等通用智能体进行对比实验，评估指标包括工具使用精确性、惩罚调整后的延迟、工具调用错误率和生成令牌成本。

Result: 专用方案在工具使用精确性上高出通用基线约9-20个百分点，惩罚调整延迟提升2-4倍，工具调用错误减少3倍，生成令牌成本降低超过95%，且无需修复迭代。通用智能体生成代码在功能和质量上不一致。

Conclusion: 在需要高可靠性和可维护性的工业场景中，专用智能体相比通用智能体具有显著优势，通用智能体不适合此类场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have accelerated the adoption of software development agents, now widely available as Integrated Development Environment (IDE) extensions and standalone applications. While these agents are typically general-purpose, it remains unclear whether specialist agents justify their additional development effort. We investigate this question in the context of business process automation, focusing on the transformation of Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) diagrams into executable agentic workflows. Since BPMN specifies explicit control-flow semantics, we focus on deterministic workflows in which a fixed process model and inputs uniquely determine the executed path. We introduce a specialist workflow for this task and compare it against generalist agents such as Roo and Cline. Our results show that the specialist solution produces agents that outperform generalist baselines by approximately 9-20 percentage points in tool-use exactness, 2-4x in penalty-adjusted latency, and 3x fewer tool-call errors, while reducing generation token cost by over 95% and eliminating repair iterations. We also find that generalist agents generate code inconsistently in both functionality and quality, limiting their suitability for industrial settings where reliability and maintainability are essential.

</details>


### [103] [Predicting Human Visual Attention on Words in Source Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14535)
*Chia-Yi Su,Collin McMillan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算模型，通过设计新颖的损失函数将人眼跟踪实验中的注意力与神经网络内部注意力对齐，来预测软件工程师阅读源代码时的视觉注意力。该模型在三个数据集（两个Java、一个C）上分别比软件工程基线提升64%、16%和467%，并超越Claude和GPT-5在阅读与写作任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程师阅读源代码时的视觉注意力反映了其认知过程，准确预测这种注意力对于优化用户界面设计和理解程序员的信息需求至关重要。

Method: 提出一种计算模型，设计新颖的损失函数来计算人类眼动追踪注意力与人工神经网络内部注意力之间的相似度，以此对齐并训练模型。

Result: 在三个数据集（两个Java、一个C）上，模型在Pearson相关系数上分别比软件工程基线提升64%、16%和467%；在阅读任务中，归一化Levenshtein距离实现统计显著的改进；在阅读和写作任务上均超越Claude和GPT-5。

Conclusion: 该模型能够有效预测程序员视觉注意力，且在需要理解人类思维过程的任务（如扫描路径预测）中表现更优，优于现有基线和大型语言模型。

Abstract: This paper presents a computational model to predict human visual attention over words in software source code. The visual attention of software engineers when reading source code has long been studied as a means to understand human cognitive processes during software engineering tasks. Predicting this visual attention is important for perfecting user interface design and understanding what information human programmers need. We propose a model of programmer visual attention in which we design a novel loss function that computes similarity between human attention measured during eye tracking experiments and the internal attention of the artificial neural network. We evaluate our model by comparing its outputs to actual eye tracking data from three separate datasets. Two are in the Java programming language and one is in the C programming language. Our model outperforms the baseline in software engineering by 64%, 16%, and 467% in each of these studies according to Pearson correlation. We used scanpath prediction as an example to demonstrate that our model is more capable of the task that requires the understanding of human thought process. Our model achieves a statistically significant improvement over the close baseline in the reading task according to normalized Levenshtein distance and outperforms both Claude and GPT-5 on both reading and writing tasks.

</details>


### [104] [A Deterministic Binary Fingerprinting Framework with Zero-Trained Feature Extraction for Sparse Count Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14596)
*Lei Zhao,Fujin Huang,Ling Kang,Quan Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MMTB是一种无需训练、确定性的二进制表示框架，通过列式Min-Max归一化和固定阈值将单细胞转录组数据映射为温度计指纹，使用汉明距离进行相似度计算。在粗粒度聚类任务上接近PCA+Leiden性能，内存约为float32表示的1/10，但在细粒度亚型发现上不及连续嵌入方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单细胞转录组数据分析依赖PCA降维、图聚类或连续嵌入空间的迭代优化，计算开销大且需要大量超参数调整。作者希望提出一种无需监督、无需模型拟合或梯度优化的确定性二进制表示方法，以降低计算和内存需求，尤其适用于资源受限场景。

Method: 对每个特征进行列式Min-Max归一化，然后使用固定阈值将连续值映射为二进制温度计指纹（thermometer fingerprint）。样本间相似度通过汉明距离计算，基于汉明最近邻图构建图结构，再使用Leiden算法进行聚类。同时提供了一个无标签的适用性分数作为部署指南。

Result: 温度计指纹的汉明距离与归一化L1距离的皮尔逊相关系数约为0.92。在三细胞系混合数据上，3阈值指纹在每细胞188字节下NMI达0.99。在粗粒度任务上接近PCA+Leiden性能，但在细粒度组织注释任务上不及连续方法（PBMC数据：MMTB NMI 0.39 vs Scanpy 0.49）。内存比float32减少约10倍。

Conclusion: MMTB适用于粗粒度分离和资源受限部署场景，而非细粒度亚型发现或连续嵌入的通用替代方案。其优势在于无需训练、内存效率高、生成固定宽度可汉明索引的编码，但不宣称通用压缩优势。

Abstract: Sparse count matrices from single-cell transcriptomes to k-mer profiles and document-term frequencies are conventionally analyzed via PCA-reduced graph clustering or iterative optimization in continuous embedding spaces. We introduce MMTB, a deterministic non-learned binary representation framework that requires no label supervision, model fitting, or gradient-based optimization. Column-wise Min-Max normalization followed by fixed cutoffs maps each sample to a thermometer fingerprint whose Hamming distances show empirical correspondence with normalized L1 distances, with Pearson correlation approximately 0.92 on single-cell RNA-seq pairs. In a favorable three-cell-line mixture, the 3-threshold fingerprint achieves NMI of 0.99 at 188 bytes per cell. Under a fair Hamming nearest-neighbor graph plus Leiden readout, MMTB approaches PCA plus Leiden on this coarse task. On challenging tissue-like annotations, continuous pipelines often lead; PBMC Seurat NMI is 0.39 for MMTB versus 0.49 for Scanpy, underscoring that MMTB is suited for coarse-grained separation and resource-constrained deployments rather than fine-grained subtype discovery or as a general replacement for continuous embeddings. Relative to dense float32 representations, MMTB fingerprints reduce memory by approximately 10-fold while providing fixed-width Hamming-indexable codes. PCA30 embeddings and sparse CSR may be smaller; we do not claim universal compression. A label-free suitability score is provided as a deployment guideline, not a performance predictor.

</details>


### [105] [SYNAPSE: A Multi-LLM Orchestrated AI Tutor for Secure Software Development Education with Neurodivergent-First Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14601)
*Giusy Ferrara,Ashkan Sami*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SYNAPSE是一个面向Java、Python网络安全和Secure Software Development的自适应辅导平台，采用Claude、GPT-4o、Gemini三模型协同（MCP协议），结合苏格拉底式提示策略和18项可访问性功能，通过ShopSecure漏洞靶场训练学习者识别-理解-修复的安全维护能力。19人可行性测试获得SUS 76.4分和4.2/5参与度，神经多样性与神经典型队列认知负荷相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业开发人员需持续识别和修复现有代码中的漏洞，但该技能缺乏直接训练；安全软件开发通常在编程熟练后才教授，且可访问性支持不足，使ADHD等执行功能差异的学习者处于劣势。

Method: SYNAPSE通过Model Context Protocol协调Claude、GPT-4o、Gemini三个大模型，按教学意图路由交互，实施三阶段苏格拉底式提示策略；内置18项始终可见的可访问性功能；基于ShopSecure（映射OWASP Top 10 2021中6类漏洞的Web应用）开展检测-理解-修复的维护实践循环。

Result: 19名参与者（含神经多样性与神经典型队列）的可行性试验：SUS可用性得分76.4，参与度评分4.2/5，两队列间认知负荷水平相当。

Conclusion: SYNAPSE在可用性和参与度方面表现良好，且神经多样性与神经典型学习者的认知负荷无显著差异，说明平台设计对认知差异具有包容性，有潜力成为安全软件开发技能训练的实用方案。

Abstract: Developers who maintain real systems must continually recognise and remediate vulnerabilities in existing code, yet this skill is rarely trained directly: secure software development is commonly taught only after programming fluency is acquired, and accessibility support is treated as a secondary concern, disadvantaging learners with ADHD and related executive-function differences. This paper presents SYNAPSE, a publicly deployed adaptive tutoring platform for Java, Python for cybersecurity, and secure software development. SYNAPSE coordinates Claude, GPT-4o, and Gemini through the Model Context Protocol, routing interactions by pedagogical intent under a three-stage Socratic hint policy. It exposes eighteen always-visible accessibility features and anchors practice in ShopSecure, a deliberately vulnerable web application mapped to six OWASP Top 10 (2021) categories, on which learners practise the detect-understand-remediate loop characteristic of software maintenance. A feasibility pilot with nineteen participants across neurodivergent and neurotypical cohorts returned a System Usability Scale score of 76.4 and engagement of 4.2/5, with comparable cognitive-load levels across cohorts. SYNAPSE is available at https://synapse-course.com; a screencast is available at https://youtu.be/9R17KC47qQI.

</details>


### [106] [Rethinking Issue Resolution for AI/ML Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14657)
*Ahmed Adnan,Mushfiqur Rahman,Antu Saha,Oscar Chaparro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对TensorFlow、scikit-learn、MLflow和AutoGPT等四个广泛使用的AI/ML系统中的100个问题报告和拉取请求进行定性研究，分析了AI/ML系统中问题解决工作流的独特特征，发现其涉及迭代实验、自适应验证、跨工件（数据集、提示词、模型配置）协调等特性，并提出了针对AI/ML系统的定制化问题解决框架愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的问题解决框架主要针对传统软件开发实践，没有明确考虑AI/ML系统的随机行为、实验驱动工作流以及源代码之外的异构工件等特性，因此需要为AI/ML系统定制专门的问题解决框架。

Method: 对TensorFlow、scikit-learn、MLflow和AutoGPT四个广泛使用的AI/ML系统中的100个问题报告和拉取请求进行定性研究，分析其中的问题解决工作流。

Result: AI/ML系统的问题解决涉及：跨多个解决阶段的重复性AI/ML相关活动；迭代实验和自适应验证；跨数据集、提示词和模型配置等工件的协调变更。同时观察到与可重现性、非确定性行为和工件协调相关的挑战。

Conclusion: 提出了AI/ML问题解决框架的愿景，并讨论了实现该愿景所需的研究方向和工具支持。

Abstract: We advocate for AI/ML issue resolution frameworks tailored to maintenance workflows and the nature of modern AI/ML systems. Existing issue resolution frameworks largely emerged for traditional software maintenance practices and do not explicitly account for characteristics common in AI/ML systems, such as stochastic behavior, experimentation-driven workflows, and heterogeneous artifacts beyond source code. To identify the unique characteristics of issue resolution in AI/ML systems and motivate the need for tailored frameworks, we conducted a qualitative study of issue resolution workflows documented in 100 issue reports and pull requests across four widely used AI/ML systems: TensorFlow, scikit-learn, MLflow, and AutoGPT. Our findings suggest that issue resolution in AI/ML systems involves: recurring AI/ML-related activities that span multiple resolution stages; iterative experimentation and adaptive verification; and coordinated changes across artifacts such as datasets, prompts, and model configurations. We also observed challenges related to reproducibility, nondeterministic behavior, and artifact coordination. Building on these findings, we present a vision for AI/ML issue resolution frameworks and discuss research directions and tooling support needed to realize this vision.

</details>


### [107] [LLM-Driven Approach to Modeling Tool Interoperability in Automotive Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14659)
*Nenad Petrovic,Jiajie Zhang,Vahid Zolfaghari,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的自动化模型互操作方法，用于解决汽车领域MDE中异构建模工具的互操作问题，涵盖模型实例到目标元模型的映射以及元模型合并两方面，通过Ecore和SysML v2的转换实验验证了方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车领域存在多种建模语言和异构工具（包括专有和开源工具），这些工具之间的互操作性一直是MDE中的重大挑战，需要自动化方法减少手动转换工作。

Method: 采用LLM驱动的方法，实现两个关键方面：1) 模型实例到目标元模型的自动映射；2) 元模型的自动合并。通过对生成模型实例进行结构化验证以确保其符合用户定义的目标模型。

Result: 通过Ecore和SysML v2元模型之间的转换实验以及汽车领域案例研究，证明LLM能够显著减少手动转换工作量，并生成结构有效的目标模型，实现跨工具互操作。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的方法可以有效解决汽车领域MDE中的异构模型互操作问题，自动化程度高且生成模型的正确性有保障。

Abstract: Interoperability between heterogeneous modeling tools remains a significant challenge in Model-Driven Engineering (MDE), particularly in the automotive domain where multiple modeling languages, as well as defacto standard proprietary and open-source tools coexist. This paper presents an LLM-driven approach for automated model interoperability by considering two relevant aspects: 1) mapping model instances to a target metamodel 2) merging of metamodels. The proposed methodology is demonstrated through transformations involving Ecore and SysML v2 based metamodels and incorporates structural validation of generated model instances against user-defined target models. Automotive case studies illustrate the feasibility of the approach and show that large language models can significantly reduce manual transformation effort while generating structurally valid target models for cross-tool interoperability.

</details>


### [108] [Large Language Models for Code Generation from Multilingual Prompts: A Curated Benchmark and a Study on Code Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14816)
*Saima Afrin,Alessandro Midolo,Camilo Escobar-Velásquez,Mario Linares-Vásquez,Weiyuan Ding,Bowen Xu,Massimiliano Di Penta,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探究了不同自然语言提示对LLM代码生成的影响，发现英语提示并非总是最优，提示语言的影响取决于编程语言和LLM模型，且生成的代码常混合英语与提示语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在不同自然语言下对相同编程任务表现不同（语言偏差）的现象已被广泛研究，但该偏差对代码生成质量和编程惯例的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 使用GPT-4o mini、DeepSeek和Claude三个模型，基于460个编程任务（Python和Java各230个），将原始英语提示翻译并人工校对为中文、印地语、西班牙语和意大利语，从功能正确性（测试通过率）、结构质量（代码度量）、静态分析工具检测问题及词法特征（标识符和注释语言）多个维度评估生成代码。

Result: (i) 英语提示并非始终产生最佳的功能正确性或代码质量；(ii) 提示语言的影响取决于编程语言和LLM模型；(iii) 生成的代码在注释和字符串字面量中经常混合英语与提示语言。

Conclusion: 提供了首个用于研究代码生成中语言偏差的精选多语言基准，为开发更稳健的多语言代码生成系统提供了见解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform differently on identical programming tasks when prompted in different natural languages, a phenomenon known as language bias. While this behavior has been widely studied for general text generation, its impact on code generation quality and programming conventions remains largely unexplored. We investigate how the language used to describe programming tasks affects the source code generated by GPT-4o mini, DeepSeek, and Claude. Our study comprises 460 coding tasks spanning Python (230) and Java (230). We translate and manually curate the original English prompts into Chinese, Hindi, Spanish, and Italian while preserving their technical meaning. We evaluate the generated code using multiple dimensions, including functional correctness through test pass rates, structural quality using established code metrics, issues detected by static analysis tools, and lexical characteristics such as the language used in identifiers and comments. Our results show that (i) English prompts do not consistently produce the best functional correctness or code quality, (ii) the impact of prompt language depends on both the programming language and the LLM, and (iii) generated code frequently mixes English with the prompt language in comments and string literals. These findings provide the first curated multilingual benchmark for studying language bias in code generation and offer insights for developing more robust multilingual code generation systems.

</details>


### [109] [AI Prototyper: A Figma Plugin for Decomposition-Based GUI Prototyping with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14830)
*Tawatchai Salangsingha,Sami Ashkan,Md Zia Ullah,Iain McGregor*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI Prototyper，一个基于分解与检索增强生成（RAG）管道的开源Figma插件，能够从自然语言描述自动生成可编辑的GUI原型，支持人机协作编辑和多语言输入，初步实验表明其比手动原型设计更高效且质量更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI原型设计仍然耗时且需要大量手工劳动，而原型作为非代码工件在整个开发周期中随需求演变，自动化其生成对软件维护和演進具有直接意义。

Method: 采用分解与检索增强生成（RAG）管道：将自然语言请求分解为离散的GUI特征，从自定义32基元库中检索匹配组件，并渲染为带有自动布局的可编辑Figma图层。使用Gemini 2.5 Flash作为LLM后端，Node.js/Express服务，并引入人在回路（human-in-the-loop）编辑步骤。

Result: 在固定时间窗口内，使用AI Prototyper的参与者完成了比手动组更多的原型；专家从业者在九个质量维度上对AI生成的原型评分更高；支持泰语、英语和中文的多语言输入。

Conclusion: AI Prototyper通过分解-RAG管道和人机协作机制，有效实现了GUI原型的自动化生成，在效率和产出质量上优于传统手动方法，且具备多语言支持能力。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) prototyping remains a time-consuming activity that demands both design expertise and considerable manual effort. As GUI prototypes are non-code artifacts that evolve alongside requirements throughout the development cycle, automating their generation is directly relevant to software maintenance and evolution. We present AI Prototyper, an open-source Figma plugin that automates GUI prototyping through a decomposition and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline. Given a natural-language description of a desired screen, such as a login page or a product detail card, the plugin decomposes the request into discrete GUI features, retrieves matching components from a custom 32-primitive library, and renders each component as a fully editable Figma layer with auto-layout. The pipeline uses Gemini 2.5 Flash as its LLM back-end and a Node.js/Express service. Unlike existing decomposition-based tools, AI Prototyper introduces a human-in-the-loop editing step that lets users review, modify, or extend the generated feature list before rendering, uses a different technology stack and LLM family, and supports multilingual input, producing correctly labelled interfaces in Thai, English, and Mandarin Chinese. In a preliminary evaluation, participants using AI Prototyper completed more prototypes in a fixed time window than those working manually, and expert practitioners rated the AI-generated prototypes higher across nine quality dimensions. A demonstration video is available at https://youtu.be/pRoFAH7MQaE. The source code and component library are available at https://github.com/tongsalangsingha/AI-prototyper-tool

</details>


### [110] [StructureClaw: Traceable LLM Agents and an Executable Benchmark for Structural Engineering Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14896)
*Sizhong Qin,Yi Gu,Yao Jiang,Ao Cai,Changjian Zhou,Shaoxuan Shuai,Jiachang Wang,Tianhao Shen,Yueqiang Li,Xinhao Li,Li Zeng,Yueshi Chen,Dachen Gao,Genrong Xu,Wenjie Liao,Xinzheng Lu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出StructureClaw，一个以工件为中心的结构工程代理工作台，以及配套基准StructureClaw-Bench。通过将LLM代理与受管控的工程技能、类型化工具、共享工件状态和本地分析后端结合，对150个受控场景进行可执行评估。在50个标准案例上，平均成功率从基线56.8%提升至88.6%。交互式和多模态评估揭示了数值输入处理和结构模型重建两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构工程请求需要完整的工件证据链（解释需求、可计算模型、验证记录、求解器输出、代码检查记录、最终报告），而现有基于问答或脚本生成的评估仅验证最终答案，无法保证底层工程工作流的完整性、内部一致性和可执行性。

Method: 提出StructureClaw工件中心工作台：LLM代理通过受管控的工程技能、类型化工具、共享工件状态和本地分析后端运作。构建StructureClaw-Bench可执行基准，包含150个受控场景（标准工作流执行、交互鲁棒性、多模态结构模型重建），场景成功需通过所有工件级和执行级断言。

Result: 在10种代理-模型配置、50个标准案例上，平均成功率从通用技能基线的56.8%提升至全自动工作流的88.6%。交互式和多模态评估指出两大挑战：无效数值输入的安全处理、夹具一致的结构模型重建。

Conclusion: 以工件为中心的评估能够暴露最终回答难以发现的工作流层级故障，为评估和改进结构工程代理提供了更严格的基准。代码和基准已开源。

Abstract: Addressing a structural-engineering request requires more than a single answer; it requires a chain of interdependent artifacts: interpreted requirements, a computable model, validation records, solver outputs, code-check records, and a final report. Evaluations centered on question answering or script generation rarely verify this complete evidence chain and may therefore reward fluent outputs even when the underlying engineering workflow is incomplete, internally inconsistent, or non-executable. To address this limitation, we present StructureClaw, an artifact-centered workbench in which LLM agents operate through governed engineering skills, typed tools, shared artifact state, and local analysis backends. We also introduce StructureClaw-Bench, an executable benchmark of 150 controlled scenarios spanning standard workflow execution, interactive robustness, and multimodal structural-model reconstruction. A scenario succeeds only when all required artifact- and execution-level assertions pass in a single run. Across ten agent-model configurations, each evaluated on the same 50 standard cases, the average Success Rate rises from 56.8% with the generic-skill baseline to 88.6% with the full automatic workflow. The interactive and multimodal evaluations identify two prominent remaining challenges: safe handling of invalid numerical inputs and fixture-consistent reconstruction of structural models. These findings show that artifact-centered evaluation can expose workflow-level failures that are difficult to identify from final responses alone, providing a more rigorous basis for evaluating and improving structural-engineering agents. The code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/structureclaw/structureclaw.

</details>


### [111] [FirmPilot: Evidence-Guided Multi-Agent Environment Recovery for IoT Firmware Rehosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14903)
*Yanbing Shen,Fan Zhang,Haitao Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FirmPilot提出了一种基于证据引导的多智能体框架，用于固件重托管中的环境恢复，通过检索驱动的搜索代理、协调执行的规划代理以及专门的工件恢复代理，迭代解决引导、持久状态和网络配置之间的跨层依赖问题。在LFwC真实固件语料库上，将Web服务可达性从25.49%提升至52.39%，网络可达性从39.30%提升至71.93%，每个固件检测到的服务数从0.86提升至1.62，并支持下游模糊测试和RouterSploit交互分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固件重托管框架（如FirmAE）在面对多样化的真实世界固件镜像时仍然脆弱，因为到达外部可观测的执行状态依赖于启动脚本、持久配置（如NVRAM键值状态）和网络设置等紧密耦合的工件。模板驱动框架难以适应长尾厂商惯例，而无约束使用大语言模型可能导致不支持的修改和不可复现的执行。

Method: FirmPilot是一个证据引导的多智能体框架，将重托管重构为迭代环境重建过程：搜索代理通过基于相似性的检索来指导决策，规划代理协调可接受的执行转换，专门的智能体分别恢复文件系统/初始化工件、持久状态和网络暴露。通过重复执行和证据引导的工件差异（artifact deltas），系统解析引导、状态和网络之间的跨层依赖关系。

Result: 在LFwC固件语料库上，FirmPilot将Web服务可达性从25.49%提升至52.39%，网络可达性从39.30%提升至71.93%；每个固件检测到的服务数从0.86提升至1.62；支持RouterSploit交互和协议感知模糊测试等下游分析工作流。

Conclusion: 证据引导和反馈驱动的代理协调能够显著提高固件重托管的成功率、服务恢复效果和下游可用性。

Abstract: Firmware rehosting executes firmware images in emulated environments such as QEMU to enable scalable dynamic analysis of Internet of Things (IoT) devices. In practice, rehosting pipelines remain fragile across diverse real-world firmware images, as reaching an externally observable execution state depends on tightly coupled artifacts spanning boot scripts, persistent configuration (e.g., NVRAM-like key-value state), and network setup. Template-driven frameworks often fail to accommodate long-tail vendor conventions, while unconstrained use of large language models (LLMs) risks unsupported modifications and irreproducible executions. We introduce FirmPilot, an evidence-guided multi-agent framework for environment recovery in firmware rehosting. FirmPilot reformulates rehosting as iterative environment reconstruction in which a search agent grounds decisions through similarity-based retrieval, a planner coordinates execution-accepted transitions, and specialized agents recover filesystem/init artifacts, persistent state, and network exposure. Through repeated execution and evidence-grounded artifact deltas, the system resolves cross-layer dependencies across boot, state, and networking that otherwise prevent firmware executions from reaching a stable, externally reachable state in emulation. Evaluated on the large-scale, real-world LFwC firmware corpus, FirmPilot improves web-service reachability over FirmAE from 25.49% to 52.39% and network reachability from 39.30% to 71.93%. The resulting rehosts raise the average number of detected services per firmware from 0.86 to 1.62 and support downstream analysis workflows, including RouterSploit interaction and protocol-aware fuzzing over recovered service surfaces. The evaluation shows that evidence- and feedback-grounded agent coordination improves rehosting success, service recovery, and downstream utility in automated firmware rehosting.

</details>


### [112] [Leaf: An Instrumentation-based Dynamic Analysis Framework for Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15025)
*Mohammad Omidvar Tehrani,Marco Gaboardi,Nick Sumner,Steven Y. Ko*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LEAF是一个面向Rust的基于插桩的动态分析框架，通过捕获MIR级语义信息并结合运行时事实，以事件驱动接口DMIR支持多种动态分析任务。通过三个具体分析（符号执行、Rust专用消毒器、控制流追踪）验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust语言快速发展，但缺乏一个通用的、支持多种分析任务的动态分析框架，现有工具生态尚不成熟。

Method: LEAF通过插桩捕获Rust的MIR级语义信息，结合运行时事实，通过Dynamic MIR (DMIR)事件驱动编程接口将信息以事件流形式传递给分析任务。

Result: 实现了三个动态分析（混合符号执行器、Rust专用消毒器、控制流追踪器），验证了框架的实用性和表达能力，编译时和运行时开销在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: LEAF填补了Rust通用动态分析框架的空白，通过暴露Rust丰富的语义信息（所有权模型、类型系统、内存模型、编译器级表示）支持多样化的动态分析任务。

Abstract: This paper presents LEAF, an instrumentation-based dynamic analysis framework for Rust. Although Rust has grown rapidly in recent years, the landscape of program analysis tools for Rust is still in relatively early stages.
